===/root/aoci-eval/repos-r5/scikit-learn/asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/===
__init__.py[TQ1T]: F:Package marker with a docstring for the ASV benchmark suite of scikit-learn. | R:- | A:- | S:-
cluster.py[TQ2S]: F:ASV benchmarks for KMeans and MiniBatchKMeans over dense and sparse inputs, scored by negative mean inertia. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:KMeansBenchmark, MiniBatchKMeansBenchmark | S:Mixin order matters; sparse uses fewer max_iter.
common.py[TQ3M]: F:Base classes and config loading for the ASV suite: Benchmark, Estimator, Predictor, Transformer, and pickled-estimator caching. | R:- | A:Benchmark, Estimator, Predictor, Transformer, get_from_config, get_estimator_path | S:Config read at import; SKLBENCH_* env overrides
datasets.py[TD3S]: F:Joblib-cached dataset builders (blobs, 20newsgroups, MNIST, digits, synthetic, faces, random) returning train/validation splits. | R:- | A:_blobs_dataset, _20newsgroups_highdim_dataset, _mnist_dataset, _synth_regression_dataset, _random_dataset | S:Disk-cached by args; clear cache after edits
decomposition.py[TQ2T]: F:ASV benchmarks for PCA, DictionaryLearning and MiniBatchDictionaryLearning on MNIST and Olivetti faces. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:PCABenchmark, DictionaryLearningBenchmark, MiniBatchDictionaryLearningBenchmark | S:-
ensemble.py[TQ2S]: F:ASV benchmarks for RandomForest, GradientBoosting and HistGradientBoosting classifiers on 20newsgroups and synthetic data. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:RandomForestClassifierBenchmark, GradientBoostingClassifierBenchmark, HistGradientBoostingClassifierBenchmark | S:-
linear_model.py[TQ2M]: F:ASV benchmarks for LogisticRegression, Ridge, LinearRegression, SGDRegressor, ElasticNet and Lasso on dense and sparse data. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:LogisticRegressionBenchmark, RidgeBenchmark, LinearRegressionBenchmark, SGDRegressorBenchmark, ElasticNetBenchmark, LassoBenchmark | S:Ridge skips sparse+svd, sparse+no-precompute
manifold.py[TQ2T]: F:ASV benchmark for TSNE (exact and barnes_hut) on the digits dataset, scored by KL divergence. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py | A:TSNEBenchmark | S:Exact method is limited to 500 samples.
metrics.py[TQ2T]: F:ASV benchmark for pairwise_distances over dense and sparse random data, several metrics and n_jobs values. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py | A:PairwiseDistancesBenchmark | S:sparse+correlation raises NotImplementedError.
model_selection.py[TQ2T]: F:ASV benchmarks for cross_val_score and GridSearchCV with a RandomForestClassifier on synthetic classification data. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:CrossValidationBenchmark, GridSearchBenchmark | S:Timeout 20000 s; large profile grows CV/grid.
neighbors.py[TP2T]: F:ASV benchmark of KNeighborsClassifier across algorithm, low/high dimension and n_jobs on the 20newsgroups low-dim dataset. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:KNeighborsClassifierBenchmark | S:low/high = n_components 10/50; 40/200 if large
svm.py[TP2T]: F:ASV benchmark of SVC across linear, poly, rbf and sigmoid kernels on a synthetic classification dataset. | R:code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/common.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/datasets.py,code:asv_benchmarks/benchmarks/utils.py | A:SVCBenchmark | S:SVC max_iter=100, tol=1e-16: times truncated fit
utils.py[TP3T]: F:Scorer helpers for ASV benchmarks: classification, regression, dictionary learning, PCA and k-means inertia metrics attached to benchmark objects. | R:- | A:make_gen_classif_scorers, make_gen_reg_scorers, make_dict_learning_scorers, make_pca_scorers, neg_mean_inertia, neg_mean_data_error | S:make_* set caller scorers; lambdas read caller.X

_splitter.pyx[KA8L]: F:Cython splitters (best/random, dense/sparse) that search features and thresholds for the best node split using a Criterion and Partitioner. | R:code:sklearn/tree/_criterion.pxd,code:sklearn/tree/_partitioner.pxd,code:sklearn/tree/_utils.pxd,code:sklearn/tree/_tree.pxd,code:sklearn/tree/_splitter.pxd | A:Splitter, BestSplitter, BestSparseSplitter, RandomSplitter, RandomSparseSplitter | S:Splitter.init drops zero-weight samples; n_samples counts only nonzero. Features with max-min <= FEATURE_THRESHOLD count as constant; ancestor constants must be restored in order via memcpy. Midpoint threshold falls back to the lower value if it rounds to upper or inf. With no training NaNs, missing_go_to_left = n_left > n_right. Best split also tries all-missing-right with threshold INFINITY; random never does.
_tree.pxd[DA7S]: F:Cython declarations of the Node and ParentInfo structs, the array-based Tree class and the TreeBuilder base class. | R:code:sklearn/tree/_tree.pyx,code:sklearn/tree/_splitter.pxd,code:sklearn/utils/_typedefs.pxd | A:Node, ParentInfo, Tree, TreeBuilder, _build_pruned_tree | S:Node layout is pickled (NODE_DTYPE derives from it), so field changes break unpickling. value is a flat (capacity, n_outputs, max_n_classes) buffer with stride value_stride.
_tree.pyx[DA9L]: F:Implements Tree storage and prediction, depth-first and best-first TreeBuilders, pickling checks, and cost-complexity pruning. | R:code:sklearn/tree/_tree.pxd,code:sklearn/tree/_splitter.pyx,code:sklearn/tree/_utils.pyx,code:sklearn/tree/_criterion.pyx | A:Tree, TreeBuilder, DepthFirstTreeBuilder, BestFirstTreeBuilder, ccp_pruning_path, _build_pruned_tree_ccp | S:Array properties are views of raw buffers: _resize or __setstate__ may free them. _add_node returns INTPTR_MAX on OOM; _resize_c(INTPTR_MAX) doubles capacity and shrinking below node_count truncates it. Node pointers go stale after any _add_node (BestFirst re-fetches). Root depth is 0 for max_depth but 1 in compute_node_depths. Only dense apply routes NaN via missing_go_to_left (decision_path and sparse apply send NaN right). Sparse paths need float32 CSR with int32 indices. Pickle accepts other bitness/endianness.
_utils.pxd[CP5S]: F:Cython declarations for safe_realloc, RNG helpers, WeightedPQueue and WeightedMedianCalculator shared by the tree code. | R:code:sklearn/tree/_utils.pyx,code:sklearn/tree/_tree.pxd | A:safe_realloc, rand_int, rand_uniform, WeightedPQueue, WeightedMedianCalculator, RAND_R_MAX | S:safe_realloc supports only pointer types in the realloc_ptr fused list, counts elements not bytes, and never frees (owner's __dealloc__ must). RAND_R_MAX is fixed at 2^31-1, not libc RAND_MAX.
_utils.pyx[CP5L]: F:Implements safe_realloc, rand_int and rand_uniform helpers, a sorted weighted priority queue, a streaming weighted median, and a NaN column check. | R:code:sklearn/tree/_utils.pxd,code:sklearn/utils/_random.pxd | A:safe_realloc, rand_int, rand_uniform, WeightedPQueue, WeightedMedianCalculator, _any_isnan_axis0 | S:WeightedPQueue push, pop and remove are O(n) shifts and push doubles capacity. get_median returns nothing if sum_w_0_k < total/2. rand_int uses modulo, so slight bias. log is base 2.
